During inference, Denoising-Stage Gated Guidance disables CFG in the final denoising steps to prevent it from amplifying quantization-induced errors into high-frequency artifacts. Extensive experiments on the Wan and CogVideoX families under W4A4 and W3A3 settings show that DSAQuant consistently outperforms the SOTA QAT baseline, improving the VBench average score by up to 6.60 under aggressive W3A3 quantization while preserving strong text-video alignment. These results demonstrate that effective VDM quantization requires not only reducing quantization error, but also aligning quantization training and inference with the stage-wise nature of video diffusion.

</details>


### [87] [TAP-Path: Task-Adaptive Structural and Token Pruning for Efficient and Trustworthy Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04071)
*Mehedi Hasan,Ashfak Yeafi,Md Khairul Islam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TAP-Path提出了一种任务自适应的压缩框架，直接重构预训练的Virchow2编码器而非蒸馏到独立学生网络。通过基于验证集的Transformer块选择、物理移除冗余块、输入自适应patch token剪枝、多深度特征恢复和轻量门控任务头，模型保留24/32个Transformer块和70%的patch token，参数量减少24.96%（631.24M→473.70M），计算量减少35.20%（340.13G→220.40G FLOPs），在32类组织病理基准上达到87.98%±0.067%的测试准确率，优于完整Virchow2（86.89%）和UNI2-h（87.67%），同时保持优异的可靠性和外部泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理学基础模型在组织病理表征学习中表现优异，但近期进展常依赖具有数亿参数和高推理成本的大规模编码器。作者旨在不损失准确性的前提下提升大型病理基础模型的效率，实现更优的准确率-效率权衡。

Method: TAP-Path框架包含五个关键组成部分：1）验证驱动的Transformer块选择（validation-driven transformer-block selection）；2）物理移除冗余块（physical removal of redundant blocks）；3）输入自适应patch token剪枝（input-adaptive patch-token pruning）；4）多深度特征恢复（multi-depth feature recovery）；5）轻量门控任务头（lightweight gated task head）。该方法直接在预训练Virchow2编码器上重构，而非蒸馏为独立学生网络。另引入基于验证集的稀有类感知目标（rare-aware objective）作为次要操作分析。

Result: 最终模型保留32个块中的24个及70%的patch token，编码器参数减少24.96%（从631.24M降至473.70M），编码器解析计算减少35.20%（从340.13G降至220.40G FLOPs）。在三个任务头优化种子上，32类组织病理基准达到87.98%±0.067%测试准确率、81.26%±0.49%平衡准确率和82.38%±0.48%宏F1，超过完整Virchow2的86.89%和UNI2-h的87.67%。Brier分数0.1800±0.0005，失败检测AUROC为0.9047±0.0060。在433个CPTAC样本上的冻结外部评估达到91.22%±0.83%准确率和91.10%±0.81%平衡准确率。

Conclusion: 任务自适应的结构和token稀疏化能够改善大型病理基础模型的准确率-效率权衡，同时在内部和外部评估中保持可靠性。TAP-Path证明了直接重构编码器的方式可以替代传统蒸馏范式，在显著减少参数和计算量的同时甚至在精度上超越原始全尺寸模型。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models improve transferable representation learning for histopathology, but recent gains often rely on encoders with hundreds of millions of parameters and high inference cost. We propose TAP-Path, a task-adaptive compression framework that directly restructures a pretrained Virchow2 encoder rather than distilling it into a separate student. TAP-Path combines validation-driven transformer-block selection, physical removal of redundant blocks, input-adaptive patch-token pruning, multi-depth feature recovery, and a lightweight gated task head. The final model retains 24 of 32 transformer blocks and 70% of patch tokens after pruning, reducing encoder parameters by 24.96% (631.24M to 473.70M) and analytical encoder compute by 35.20% (340.13G to 220.40G FLOPs). Across three task-head optimization seeds, TAP-Path achieved $87.98 \pm 0.067%$ test accuracy, $81.26 \pm 0.49%$ balanced accuracy, and $82.38 \pm 0.48%$ macro-F1 on a 32-class histopathology benchmark, compared with 86.89% for full Virchow2 and 87.67% for UNI2-h. TAP-Path achieved a Brier score of $0.1800 \pm 0.0005$ and failure-detection AUROC of $0.9047 \pm 0.0060$. A validation-only rare-aware objective improved rare-class balanced accuracy in a secondary operating analysis. Frozen external evaluation on 433 CPTAC samples yielded $91.22 \pm 0.83%$ accuracy and $91.10 \pm 0.81%$ balanced accuracy. These results show that task-adaptive structural and token sparsification can improve the accuracy-efficiency trade-off of large pathology foundation models while preserving reliability under internal and external evaluation.

</details>


### [88] [Editable Visual Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04034)
*Junyan Ye,Wei Liu,Dongzhi Jiang,Zichen Wen,HaoDong Li,Zhutao Lv,Jiaxin Lin,Jinhua Yu,Jun He,Zilong Huang,Rui Chen,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"可编辑视觉设计"(Editable Visual Design)范式，由Coding Agent驱动，将VLM作为创意大脑负责理解需求、规划任务和审美评判，图像生成模型作为按需的"视觉世界模拟器"生成独立视觉素材，通过"先想象后行动"的闭环流程生成可编辑的HTML/CSS设计，实现分层可编辑和真实文本，兼具美学要求与生产级可编辑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两类视觉生成方法各有局限：扩散基础模型（如GPT-Image-2、Nano-Banana）虽然视觉表现力强，但端到端生成产生扁平的位图，文本易出错且无法进行分层后期编辑；而基于代码的视觉生成（Coding Agents）虽然能精确控制布局和解耦层，但缺乏全局美学直觉且难以编码复杂视觉素材。因此需要一种新范式同时兼顾美学质量与可编辑性。

Method: 核心方法是"Editable Visual Design"范式，由Coding Agent驱动。具体机制为：(1) 将VLM设计为"创意大脑"，负责需求理解、任务规划和审美判断；(2) 将图像生成模型用作按需的"视觉世界模拟器"以合成独立视觉素材；(3) 在"先想象、后行动"的闭环工作流下，Agent生成独立素材、编写原生HTML/CSS，并根据视觉渲染反馈迭代优化设计；(4) 引入Agent Design Replay来忠实复现专业人类设计师的创作与推理轨迹。最终系统交付具有解耦层和真实文本的可编辑产物，支持用户在图形界面上拖拽和布局调整。

Result: 在海报、信息图表等场景上的验证表明，该范式能够同时实现精细的美学效果和具备生产级的可编辑性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明通过代码代理将VLM的创意能力与图像生成模型的视觉合成能力相结合是可行的，能够在保留优秀美学质量的同时提供解耦的分层结构和真实文本，使得最终设计产物具备直观交互的生产级可编辑性，弥补了扩散模型与纯代码生成两条路线的各自不足。

Abstract: While diffusion base models such as GPT-Image-2 and Nano-Banana exhibit remarkable visual expressiveness, their end-to-end generation inherently yields flattened bitmaps with error-prone text, precluding layer-wise post-editing. Conversely, code-based visual generation via Coding Agents provides precise layout control and decoupled layers, yet remains constrained by a lack of global aesthetic intuition and the difficulty of coding complex visual assets.
  To address this, we propose Editable Visual Design, a new paradigm driven by a Coding Agent. We designate the VLM as the ``creative brain'' for requirement comprehension, task planning, and aesthetic judgment, while utilizing the image generation model as an on-demand ``visual world simulator'' to synthesize standalone visual assets. Operating under an ``imagine first, then act'' closed-loop workflow, the agent generates isolated assets, writes native HTML/CSS, and iteratively refines the design against visual rendering feedback.
  Furthermore, Agent Design Replay faithfully reproduces the creative and reasoning trajectory akin to that of professional human designers. Ultimately, the system delivers editable artifacts with decoupled layers and real text, enabling users to perform intuitive mouse dragging and layout adjustments on a graphical user interface. Validations on posters, infographics, and other scenarios show that this paradigm successfully achieves both refined aesthetics and production-grade editability.

</details>


### [89] [CORE: Improving Compositional Reasoning in MLLM Embedding via Reranker Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04083)
*Tingyu Song,Mingxin Li,Yanzhao Zhang,Dingkun Long,Chu Liu,Pengjun Xie,Yilun Zhao,Shu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CORE方法，通过将MLLM重排器（reranker）的组合性判断蒸馏到嵌入模型（embedding model）中，解决组合检索任务中属性-客体绑定区分能力不足的问题。采用五种组合匹配级别合成候选列表，引入Rank-KL损失函数训练嵌入模型复现重排器的细粒度排序，在多个组合推理基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于MLLM的嵌入模型在组合检索中表现受限，难以区分包含相同概念但属性-客体绑定不同的场景。然而同一骨干网络用作交叉注意力重排器时却能解决此类区分，这启发作者将其组合性判断蒸馏到嵌入模型中，以弥补嵌入模型在该任务上的短板。

Method: 提出CORE框架：1）合成覆盖五种组合匹配级别的候选列表（从完全匹配到完全失配）；2）引入Rank-KL目标，训练嵌入模型以复现交叉注意力重排器对候选的细粒度排序；3）在同一数据和调优预算下对比对比学习、逐对CoSENT和列表级Rank-KL三种训练范式；4）提出分级（graded）评估协议以更精细地衡量组合检索能力。

Result: 在COLA、SUGARCREPE++、NEGBENCH三个组合推理基准上，CORE-RERANKER-8B达到82.7%的总平均分，比Jina-Reranker高10.7个百分点；CORE-EMBED-8B在所有评估的嵌入模型中取得最佳总平均分0.666。CoSENT和Rank-KL比对比学习更有效地利用多级监督，其中Rank-KL整体表现最强；改进还能迁移到MCMR基准，且不牺牲COCO和Flickr30K上的检索性能。

Conclusion: 列表级Rank-KL蒸馏是将重排器组合性判断转移到嵌入模型的最有效方式，证明从交叉注意力重排器到密集嵌入模型的组合性知识蒸馏能够显著提升组合检索的绑定区分能力，同时保持传统检索性能不回退。

Abstract: MLLM-based embedding models remain limited in compositional retrieval, often failing to distinguish scenes containing the same concepts but different attribute-object bindings. Yet the same backbone can resolve such distinctions when used as a cross-attentive reranker, motivating us to distill its compositional judgments into the embedding model. We propose CORE, which synthesizes candidate lists spanning five compositional matching levels and introduces a Rank-KL objective that trains the embedding model to reproduce the reranker's fine-grained ranking. We further introduce a graded evaluation protocol and compare contrastive learning, pairwise CoSENT, and listwise Rank-KL under the same data and tuning budget. Our comparison shows that both CoSENT and Rank-KL use the multi-level supervision more effectively than contrastive learning, with Rank-KL achieving the strongest overall performance. Across three compositional reasoning benchmarks (COLA, SUGARCREPE++, NEGBENCH), CORE-RERANKER-8B achieves an 82.7% total average, outperforming Jina-Reranker by 10.7 points, while CORE-EMBED-8B achieves the best total average (0.666) among all evaluated embedding models. The improvements transfer to the MCMR benchmark without sacrificing retrieval performance on COCO and Flickr30K.

</details>


### [90] [Seeing Before Synthesizing: VLM-Guided Transition Event Discovery for Weakly-Supervised Dense Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04183)
*Ye-Chan Kim,Seunghee Choi,SeungJu Cha,Si-Woo Kim,Hwiseon Kim,Hyungee Kim,Dong-Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SBS框架，通过VLM生成帧级叙事并检测语义变化点来确定过渡事件，解决弱监督密集视频描述中合成过渡字幕缺乏视觉依据和位置固定分配的问题，在ActivityNet Captions和YouCook2上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱监督密集视频描述方法利用LLM合成辅助过渡字幕以增强视觉-语言对齐，但这些合成字幕缺乏视觉依据（grounding），且被机械地分配到每个事件间间隙的固定位置和时长，不符合真实场景中过渡事件的灵活性和语义性。

Method: 提出Seeing Before Synthesizing (SBS)框架：(1) 利用VLM为事件间间隙生成帧级叙事；(2) 通过帧级叙事的语义变化检测过渡事件（transition）；(3) 对识别出的过渡事件，融合时间中点与语义变化点来精炼事件间的时间掩码，并选择最大化视觉-语言对齐的宽度。

Result: 在ActivityNet Captions和YouCook2数据集上与现有方法相比，在描述（captioning）和定位（localization）两方面均达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过"先看后合成"的策略，仅在必要时提供具有视觉依据的语言指导，相比盲目分配合成字幕的方法更有效，验证了适应性、语义驱动的过渡事件建模对弱监督密集视频描述的价值。

Abstract: Weakly-Supervised Dense Video Captioning aims to localize and describe multiple events in untrimmed videos given only an ordered set of event-level captions per video. Recent work synthesizes auxiliary transition captions via LLM to provide additional vision-language alignment, but these captions lack visual grounding and are rigidly assigned to every inter-event gap at a fixed location and duration. To address these, we propose Seeing Before Synthesizing (SBS), a framework that adaptively provides visually grounded linguistic guidance only where warranted. Leveraging a VLM, we generate frame-level narratives for the inter-event gaps and detect transitions from the semantic variation across them. For identified transitions, we then refine inter-event temporal masks by blending the temporal midpoint with the semantic change point and selecting the width that maximizes vision-language alignment. Experiments on ActivityNet Captions and YouCook2 demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in both captioning and localization.

</details>


### [91] [One Editor, Many Edits: A Unified Training-Free Framework for Diverse Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04190)
*Adheesh Sunil Juvekar,Onkar Kishor Susladkar,Kiet A. Nguyen,Muntasir Wahed,Nabeel Bashir,Xiaona Zhou,Tianjiao Yu,Vedant Shah,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EditVid是一个无需训练的统一的视频编辑框架，结合稀疏因果记忆、基于对应关系的注意力后令牌注入和软潜在混合，同时支持指令引导和参考引导编辑，在FiVE基准上取得78.16的FiVE-Acc成绩，显著优于最强训练无关基线（58.95），用户研究中获得51.8%的总体偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频编辑涵盖多种编辑范式（指令引导、主题引导等），但现有的方法往往针对单一范式设计，难以在统一框架中同时实现高质量的指令引导和主题引导编辑。

Method: 提出EditVid，一个无需训练的统一框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）稀疏因果记忆用于保持局部连贯性；（2）基于对应关系的注意力后令牌注入用于长距离身份保持；（3）软潜在混合用于保证编辑局部性（只影响目标区域而不破坏原视频其他部分）。同一框架可同时支持指令引导和参考引导的多种编辑类型。

Result: 在FiVE数据集上取得78.16的FiVE-Acc，远超最强训练无关基线的58.95；在IVEBench上取得具有竞争力的结果；用户研究中获51.8%总体偏好，优于7种对比方法。

Conclusion: EditVid证明了无需训练即可在一个统一框架中同时支持指令引导和主题引导的视频编辑，且在各编辑范式中均达到或超越现有训练无关方法的性能。

Abstract: Video editing spans diverse editing paradigms, yet achieving high-quality instruction-guided and subject-guided editing within a single unified framework remains challenging. We introduce EditVid, a training-free framework combining sparse causal memory for local coherence, correspondence-based post-attention token injection for long-range identity preservation, and soft latent blending for edit locality. The same framework supports instruction-guided and reference-guided edits, including style transfer, attribute modification, object insertion, part-level editing, and subject replacement. On FiVE, EditVid achieves 78.16 FiVE-Acc, compared with 58.95 for the strongest evaluated training-free baseline, while obtaining competitive results on IVEBench. A user study further shows a 51.8\% overall preference for EditVid over 7 competing methods.

</details>


### [92] [Efficient Semantic Understanding from Digital Foveation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04088)
*Caterina Caccavella,Vittorio Fra,Andreas Ziegler,Giulia D'Angelo,Yulia Sandamirskaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种受生物视觉启发的主动视觉（digital foveated perception）轻量级流水线，通过显著性驱动的注视点选择、高分辨率中央凹感知与低分辨率上下文信息、语义累积和自适应计算来实现高效语义分割，在显著节省算力的情况下保持较高的语义理解精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统密集语义分割对整个图像均匀分配计算资源，忽略场景复杂度和任务相关性，效率低下。作者从生物视觉（中央凹感知）获得灵感，探索能否通过选择性计算（主动视觉）而非均匀密集处理来实现高效语义理解。

Method: 提出一个轻量级主动视觉流水线，包含四个关键组件：1）显著性驱动的注视点（fixation）选择机制确定何处分配高分辨率；2）高分辨率中央凹（foveal）观测用于局部细节理解；3）低分辨率上下文信息提供全局场景线索；4）语义累积与自适应计算逐步整合稀疏观测结果。评估方法上，除传统密集预测指标外，还引入物体级评估来衡量稀疏观测下的语义理解能力。

Result: 在ADE20K-Object数据集上：单次中央凹观测达到基线Top-1精度的95.9%和Top-3精度的96.9%，但仅需4.7%的计算成本；场景层面通过语义累积可恢复基线物体召回率的90.6%，同时仅使用58.6%的计算量。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当计算资源被选择性分配时，稀疏观测足以产生显著的语义理解能力。主动视觉可作为均匀密集处理的高效替代方案，并推动超越传统像素级分割指标的新的评估协议开发。

Abstract: Dense semantic segmentation allocates computational resources uniformly across the entire image, regardless of scene complexity or task relevance. Inspired by biological vision, we investigate whether semantic understanding can be achieved more efficiently through digital foveated perception. We introduce a lightweight active-vision pipeline that combines saliency-driven fixation selection, high-resolution foveal observations, low-resolution contextual information, semantic accumulation, and adaptive computation. Beyond conventional dense prediction metrics, we use object-level evaluation to measure semantic understanding under sparse observations. On ADE20K-Object, a single foveated observation achieves 95.9% of the baseline Top-1 accuracy and 96.9% of the baseline Top-3 accuracy while requiring only 4.7% of the computational cost. At the scene level, semantic accumulation recovers 90.6% of the baseline object recall while using 58.6% of the computation. These results suggest that substantial semantic understanding can emerge from sparse observations when computation is allocated selectively, highlighting active vision as an efficient alternative to uniform dense processing and motivating evaluation protocols beyond conventional pixel-wise segmentation metrics.

</details>


### [93] [The Shape of Time: Video-Token Contrast for Temporal Understanding in VideoLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04110)
*Yumeng Shi,Quanyu Long,Yin Wu,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VT-Contrast，一种面向视频语言模型的表示级时间反事实目标函数，通过在生成前对视频token表示施加时间顺序监督，迫使模型真正理解事件动态而非仅依赖文本或静态线索捷径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视频语言模型虽然接收有序的视频帧流，但主要训练监督作用于生成的文本而非视频token表示，导致模型可通过物体、场景和语言先验等捷径学习时间相关答案，而不需要在内部视频表示中真正捕捉事件进展。

Method: VT-Contrast是一个表示级时间反事实目标，在选定的晚期层最后一帧视频token上施加监督（此处预期时间信息在语言生成前集成），并将保序视图与同一视频打乱顺序的反事实视图进行对比，反事实程度按Kendall tau距离分档。无需架构改动，可与多种VideoLM训练任务兼容。

Result: 该目标无需架构修改，兼容多种VideoLM训练任务，并在多个时间理解基准上整体提升了性能。

Conclusion: VT-Contrast通过将时间监督从文本输出下移到视频token表示层，有效缓解了模型依赖捷径学习时间答案的问题，验证了时间监督应作用于表示层且反事实对比设计的重要性。

Abstract: Seeing frames in order does not mean representing time. Modern VideoLMs receive ordered video streams, yet their main supervision acts on generated text rather than video-token representations where event dynamics should first emerge. This mismatch allows models to learn temporal answers from shortcuts such as objects, scenes, and language priors, without requiring internal video representations to capture event progression. To address this, we propose VT-Contrast, a representation-level temporal counterfactual objective for VideoLMs. Its design asks where temporal supervision should act and what temporal differences it should expose. VT-Contrast supervises selected late-layer last-frame video tokens, where temporal information is expected to be integrated before language generation, and contrasts order-preserving views with same-video reordered counterfactuals graded by Kendall tau distance. It requires no architectural changes, is compatible with diverse VideoLM training tasks, and improves overall performance across temporal understanding benchmarks. Our code is available at https://github.com/ANDgate99/VT-Contrast.

</details>


### [94] [BooM-VVT: Boosting Mask-Free Video Virtual Try-On with Image-Level Pseudo Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04120)
*Wei Zhang,Xin Li,Peishu Shi,Jialin Gao,Xuekang Peng,Zhichao Lian,Yeying Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BooM-VVT是一个无掩码(mask-free)的视频虚拟试穿框架，基于关键帧驱动范式，通过多阶段训练策略利用图像级伪数据进行无掩码定位学习，提出服装敏感关键帧采样和帧共享3D-RoPE增强服装一致性与时空对应，并构建大规模多视角试穿数据集OmniView以支持复杂视角和多样化试穿任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频虚拟试穿(VVT)方法依赖掩码定位试穿区域，在大幅度运动和严重遮挡下表现脆弱；无掩码图像级方法虽表现良好但难以扩展到视频（视频级伪数据构建成本过高）；且现有关键帧驱动方法受限于粗粒度关键帧采样和多视角数据稀缺，难以维持服装一致性并处理多样化试穿任务。

Method: 1) 多阶段训练策略：利用图像级伪数据进行无掩码定位学习，减少对昂贵视频级伪数据的依赖；2) Garment-Sensitive Keyframe Sampling：基于服装相关身体区域选取关键帧以更好捕捉服装外观；3) Frame-Shared 3D-RoPE：建立关键帧与目标视频帧间的时空对应关系，实现精确的服装细节迁移；4) 构建OmniView大规模多视角试穿数据集，支持复杂摄像头视角下可靠的视频生成。

Result: 大量实验表明，BooM-VVT在时间一致性和服装保真度上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: BooM-VVT通过无掩码设计与关键帧驱动范式的结合，解决了视频虚拟试穿中遮挡、运动、服装一致性及多视角数据稀缺等核心挑战，是该领域的一个有效进展。

Abstract: Video virtual try-on (VVT) aims to generate realistic videos of a person wearing a target garment. Recent methods leverage a keyframe-driven video generation paradigm to improve in-the-wild performance, yet they still rely on masks to localize try-on regions, making them vulnerable to large motions and severe occlusions. Although mask-free image-based try-on methods have shown promising results by leveraging large-scale pseudo data, extending this paradigm to videos remains difficult, as constructing video-level pseudo data is prohibitively expensive. Furthermore, coarse keyframe sampling and the scarcity of multi-view try-on data limit existing keyframe-driven methods in maintaining garment consistency and handling diverse try-on tasks.
  To address these challenges, we propose BooM-VVT, a mask-free VVT framework built upon the keyframe-driven paradigm. To achieve mask-free VVT, we introduce a multi-stage training strategy that leverages image-level pseudo data for mask-free localization learning, substantially reducing the need for costly video-level pseudo data. To improve garment consistency, we propose Garment-Sensitive Keyframe Sampling, which selects keyframes based on garment-relevant body regions to better capture garment appearance. We further introduce Frame-Shared 3D-RoPE to establish spatiotemporal correspondences between keyframes and target video frames for accurate garment-detail transfer. Finally, we construct OmniView, a large-scale multi-view try-on dataset to support reliable try-on video generation under complex camera viewpoints and diverse try-on tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BooM-VVT achieves superior temporal consistency and garment fidelity over existing methods. Project page: https://boomvvt.github.io/boomvvt.

</details>


### [95] [Beyond Retrieval: Progressive Latent Memory Evolution for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04131)
*Hongyu Qu,Guangming Yao,Ling Xing,Xiaobin Hu,Rongxing Ding,Guibin Zhang,Fan Zhang,Yi Yuan,Xiangbo Shu,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LatentStream提出了一种渐进式潜变量工作记忆框架，将流式视频理解中的记忆机制从"存储-检索"范式转变为"检索-内化"范式，通过分层潜记忆演化与置信度引导优化，在在线和离线视频基准上取得新的最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流式视频理解方法通常采用外部记忆库存储历史观测，并在回答查询时检索相关证据作为额外的视觉上下文。然而这种"存储-检索"范式将历史证据保持为外部视觉上下文，无法将其内化为紧凑、演化的潜记忆来持续引导流式推理，存在记忆压缩与推理引导之间的本质鸿沟。

Method: LatentStream包含三个协同组件：(1) 查询无关的分层流式记忆（Query-agnostic Hierarchical Streaming Memory），通过Jenks引导的自适应整合，在固定记忆预算下将视觉历史组织为短期、中期和长期三个层级；(2) 分层潜记忆演化（Hierarchical Latent Memory Evolution），为潜记忆令牌组配备逐渐扩展的记忆感受野，使其能够迭代地从各自对应范围检索历史证据并将其内化为紧凑的固定长度潜记忆；(3) 渐进式置信度引导的潜记忆优化（Progressive Confidence-guided Latent Memory Optimization），通过组级预测熵构建分层进度奖励，联合优化潜记忆令牌与检索证据，鼓励更自信的流式推理。

Result: 大量实验表明，LatentStream在现有的在线和离线视频理解基准上均取得了新的最先进（state-of-the-art）结果。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了将历史记忆从外部"存储-检索"范式转变为内部"检索-内化"范式的有效性，为流式视频理解中的记忆建模提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to process continuous visual inputs and respond to user queries under strict causality and bounded memory. Existing approaches typically compress historical observations into an external memory bank and retrieve query-relevant evidence as additional visual context. Though effective, this store-and-retrieve paradigm keeps historical evidence as external visual context, preventing it from being internalized into a compact, evolving latent memory that can continuously guide streaming reasoning. To bridge this gap, we introduce LatentStream, a progressive latent working memory framework that shifts streaming memory from store-and-retrieve to retrieve-and-internalize. Specifically, LatentStream comprises three coordinated components. First, Query-agnostic Hierarchical Streaming Memory organizes visual history into short-, mid-, and long-term levels under a fixed memory budget through Jenks-guided adaptive consolidation. Once a query arrives, Hierarchical Latent Memory Evolution equips groups of latent memory tokens with progressively expanding memory receptive fields, enabling them to iteratively retrieve historical evidence from their corresponding scopes and internalize it into a compact, fixed-length latent memory. Finally, Progressive Confidence-guided Latent Memory Optimization constructs a hierarchical progression reward from group-wise predictive entropy and jointly refines the latent memory tokens and retrieved evidence, encouraging increasingly confident streaming reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LatentStream achieves new state-of-the-art results on existing online and offline video benchmarks.

</details>


### [96] [Persistent Identity Preservation in Generative Image Models: A Benchmark and Evaluation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04151)
*Mengwei Ren,Xuaner Zhang,Zhihao Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了三种主体驱动范式（输入上下文、可训练模型参数、持久身份图层）在图像生成、编辑、修复及多主体场景下的身份保持能力，发现当前生成基础模型的强图像质量与指令遵循并不必然带来强身份保真度，而使用可跨生成与编辑复用的持久身份（PHOTA IDENTITY）能显著降低身份漂移，且不损害指令遵循与感知图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式图像模型虽然擅长高质量图像生成、复杂指令遵循与精确编辑，但在保持所描绘主体的身份一致性方面仍存在明显不足。随着姿态、表情、外观、视角或场景变化，身份会漂移。现有主体驱动方法在身份表示位置上有根本性分歧（输入上下文、可训练参数或持久身份层），缺乏系统性的对比基准来理解各范式的优势与局限。

Method: 作者对三种主体驱动范式——输入上下文方法（GPT-Image-2、NB2）、可训练主体特定参数（LoRA）、持久身份层（PHOTA IDENTITY）——进行了系统化基准测试。评测覆盖主体驱动生成、编辑、修复、多主体设定等任务，并设计了逐步增加身份保持难度的任务（如迭代编辑、小主体尺度、严重图像退化、多主体组合）。

Result: 实验表明：强图像质量与指令遵循并不必然意味着强主体身份保真；身份退化在迭代编辑、小主体尺度、严重图像退化及多主体组合场景下更为显著。持久身份表示在生成、编辑与修复任务中均能稳定降低身份退化，在不同基础模型上应用时均能一致改善身份保持，同时保持相当的指令遵循能力与感知图像质量。

Conclusion: 身份保真并非从日益强大的生成模型中自然涌现，而是可以表示为与底层生成模型独立组合的持久主体知识。这为未来模型设计提供了新方向：将身份作为可复用的显式组件而非隐含涌现的能力来建模。

Abstract: Generative image models can now produce high-quality images, follow complex instructions, and support precise edits, but they still struggle to preserve who or what is being depicted. When generating or editing images of a specific subject, identity may drift as the pose, expression, appearance, viewpoint, or surrounding scene changes. Existing subject-driven methods make fundamentally different choices about where identity is represented: through the input context (GPT-Image-2, NB2), as trainable subject-specific model parameters (LoRA), or as a persistent identity layer (PHOTA IDENTITY) reusable across generations and edits. We systematically benchmark these paradigms across subject-driven generation, editing, restoration, and multi-subject settings, with tasks designed to increasingly stress identity preservation. Our results show that identity preservation remains a distinct limitation of current generative foundation models: strong image quality and instruction following do not necessarily imply strong identity fidelity, and identity degradation becomes more pronounced under iterative edits, small subject scales, severe image degradation, and multi-subject composition. Persistent identity substantially reduces this degradation across generation, editing, and restoration, consistently improving identity preservation when applied to different foundation models while maintaining comparable instruction adherence and perceptual image quality. These results suggest that identity does not simply emerge from increasingly capable generative models, but can instead be represented as persistent subject knowledge that is composed independently with the underlying generative model.

</details>


### [97] [Zero-Shot Novel Depth Synthesis Using 3D Foundation Models Scene Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04174)
*Denis M. Akola,David F. Fouhey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Z3D方法，利用3D基础模型（如VGGT）的内部表征，通过潜在扩散模型在表征空间中进行生成，从而推断未见视角下的3D场景结构（点图与深度图）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D基础模型（3DFMs）在内部表征中蕴含了大量关于3D场景的通用知识，作者假设这些表征可用于推断新视角下的3D结构（如被遮挡的隐藏表面），并探索如何将这些内部表征用于从未见视角生成3D信息。

Method: 首先验证可以从3DFM内部表征解码隐藏表面；随后提出Z3D方法，即在3DFM表征空间上执行潜在扩散（latent diffusion），以估计未见视角下的点图（pointmaps），从而生成逼真的深度图。

Result: Z3D能够在多个数据集上预测新视角下逼真的深度图，验证了利用3DFM内部表征进行跨视角3D推断的有效性。

Conclusion: 3DFM的内部表征包含丰富的3D场景先验知识，通过潜在扩散的方式可以有效解码出新视角的3D结构，为基于基础模型的新视角3D生成提供了新的思路。

Abstract: 3D Foundation Models (3DFMs) such as VGGT have recently pushed the boundaries of 3D vision by predicting rich unified representations with feed-foward transformers. The scene representations learned by these models enable strong performance on multiple 3D vision tasks. In this paper, we investigate using their internal representations to infer 3D in the scene from new views. Our hypothesis is that in order to solve the task of 3D reconstruction, these models need to learn a representation that includes a large amount of general knowledge about 3D scenes. After showing that it is possible to decode hidden surfaces from internal 3DFM representations, we propose a method, Z3D, that estimates pointmaps in unseen views by doing latent diffusion on 3DFM representation. We show that Z3D can predict realistic depth maps for new views across multiple datasets.

</details>


### [98] [Puffin-World: Scaling a Unified Multimodal Model with Native 3D World States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04196)
*Kang Liao,Yihang Luo,Xiao-Ming Wu,Linyi Jin,Size Wu,Chunyu Lin,Yao Zhao,Fei Wang,Wei Li,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Puffin-World，一个统一的多模态架构，在不依赖外部离线模块的情况下，整合物理理解、空间模拟与3D世界生成/重建。其联合建模物理（重力场与纬度）、几何（深度）与外观（图像）三种原生世界状态，并提出统一Omni-Camera表示以支持多样任务与灵活运动。通过将相机绝对属性锚定于真实世界并在未来帧间传播物理动力学，实现了物理一致且视觉稳定的世界生成，并支持多项任务协同的闭环应用与自校准世界探索。论文还构建了含1500万视觉-语言-相机三元组和100万轨迹的Puffin-16M数据集并开源代码、模型与数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D世界理解与生成方法通常依赖外部离线模块，割裂了物理理解、空间模拟与3D生成重建之间的内在联系，难以实现物理一致且支持多任务协同的复合闭环应用。作者旨在构建一个内在统一的多模态范式，使模型能同时胜任物理建模与3D世界交互。

Method: 设计统一架构联合建模三种原生世界状态：物理（重力场与纬度）、几何（深度）、外观（图像）；引入统一的Omni-Camera表示以适配多种任务和灵活相机运动；提出跨未来帧传播物理动力学的策略，并将相机绝对属性锚定于真实世界以确保物理一致性；在单一生成过程中耦合外观与几何，同时合成未来视角并重建其底层几何。

Result: 构建Puffin-16M大规模数据集（1500万视觉-语言-相机三元组与100万条轨迹），覆盖多样且富有挑战性的运动；在物理一致的世界生成和几何重建上取得了稳健表现，支持模拟与自校准探索等交叉任务的闭环应用。

Conclusion: Puffin-World通过原生统一的世界状态建模与物理锚定，为物理理解、空间模拟与3D世界生成重建提供了一种闭环自洽的范式，并开源代码、模型与数据集以推动该方向研究。

Abstract: We propose Puffin-World, a unified multimodal architecture that integrates physical understanding, spatial simulation, and 3D world generation and reconstruction without relying on external offline modules. To reliably construct and interact with 3D worlds, our framework jointly models three native world states: physics (gravity field and latitude), geometry (depth), and appearance (image), together with a unified Omni-Camera representation that supports diverse tasks and flexible motions. Beyond modeling these states, we introduce a strategy for propagating physical dynamics across future frames. By grounding absolute camera properties in the real world, Puffin-World enables physically consistent and visually stable world generation. We further couple appearance and geometry within a single generative process, jointly synthesizing each future view and reconstructing its underlying geometry. This unified paradigm enables interleaved closed-loop applications requiring synergy across multiple tasks, including mimic and self-calibrated world exploration. To scale Puffin-World to complex scenarios, we construct Puffin-16M, comprising 15 million vision-language-camera triplets and 1 million trajectories featuring various and challenging motions. To foster further research in this area, we released the code, models, and datasets.

</details>


### [99] [Principia: Relational Physics Tests for Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04200)
*Varun Varma Thozhiyoor,Shivam Tripathi,Venkatesh Babu Radhakrishnan,Anand Bhattad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Principia基准，通过检验同一场景中两个物体运动间的物理关系一致性来评估视频模型的牛顿物理推理能力，该方法是校准无关的。在六个最先进的视频生成模型上，所有模型得分均不超过0.42，而VBench得分约0.8；视觉语言模型检测物理违规的最佳准确率仅67%，多数接近随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估视频模型中的物理推理一直很困难，因为绝对运动测量依赖于帧率、物体尺度和相机校准，这些因素在生成视频中常不明确或不可用。现有基准（如VBench）无法有效衡量模型的物理理解能力，需要一种不依赖校准的评估方法。

Method: 提出一种基于关系一致性的方法：当同一场景中的两个物体遵循相同物理定律时，它们的运动必须满足可预测的关系，并且这些关系与校准无关。基于此构建Principia基准，覆盖八种物理现象（重力、恢复系数、摩擦、转动惯量、抛体运动、动量、单摆和弹簧振子），涵盖平移、转动、碰撞和振荡四类动力学。同时引入一种校准无关的一致性评分，直接在图像空间中量化物理违规程度。

Result: 在六个最先进的视频生成模型的数千次生成中，没有模型在Principia上超过0.42分，尽管所有这些模型在VBench上均得分约0.8。对于视觉语言模型检测关系性物理违规的能力，最佳模型仅达到67%准确率，大多数模型表现接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 现有视频生成模型在物理推理方面表现严重不足，即使它们在传统视频评估基准上得分很高；视觉语言模型对物理违规的检测能力也非常有限。这揭示了当前模型对基础物理规律理解存在显著缺陷，Principia提供了一个更有效的评估工具来揭示这一差距。

Abstract: Evaluating physical reasoning in video models is difficult because absolute motion measurements depend on frame rate, object scale, and camera calibration, all of which are often ambiguous or unavailable in generated video. We propose a different approach. When two objects in the same scene obey the same physical law, their motions must satisfy predictable relationships, and these relationships hold independent of calibration. We introduce Principia, a benchmark that evaluates Newtonian physics through relational consistency between paired objects. Principia spans eight phenomena - gravity, restitution, friction, rotational inertia, projectile motion, momentum, pendulum, and mass-spring oscillation - across translational, rotational, collisional, and oscillatory dynamics, using real-world scenes recorded under controlled protocols. We also introduce a calibration-independent consistency score that quantifies physical violation directly in image space. Across thousands of generations from six state-of-the-art video generators, no model exceeds 0.42 on Principia despite all scoring around 0.8 on VBench. Vision-language models are evaluated on their ability to detect relational physics violations, with the best model achieving only 67% accuracy and most performing near chance level.

</details>


### [100] [Scal3R: Learning Efficient Multi-Relative Pose Query for Scalable Online 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04201)
*Chin-Yang Lin,Yang-Che Sun,Cheng Sun,Fu-En Yang,Min-Hung Chen,Yen-Yu Lin,Wei-Chen Chiu,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Scal3R，一种用于长视频在线3D重建的方法，通过将重建问题重构为多参考相对位姿查询，利用轻量级可学习token注入冻结骨干网络，并结合在线位姿图优化和闭环来抑制长期漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线3D重建模型在长视频上表现不佳，因为相对于固定首帧锚点回归位姿会迫使模型外推远超出训练分布，微小漂移不断累积放大为严重的几何坍塌。

Method: 观察到逐帧深度在重建失败时保持稳定，即骨干网络的局部几何完好，只有全局位姿头出现问题。基于这种解耦观察，Scal3R将在线重建重构为多参考相对位姿查询：使用约占参数1%的轻量级可学习token，通过非对称注意力注入完全冻结的骨干网络，查询相对于多个历史关键帧的位姿；并采用带闭环的在线位姿图优化系统抑制长期漂移。

Result: Scal3R在单张GPU上8小时即可收敛；在KITTI上相比在线基线平均ATE降低超过60%；在Virtual KITTI、Sintel、TUM-Dynamic、ScanNet和7-Scenes等数据集上均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 通过将全局位姿回归与局部几何解耦，多参考相对位姿查询配以位姿图优化能有效解决在线3D重建的长期漂移问题，且计算开销极低、收敛快速。

Abstract: Online 3D reconstruction models perform poorly on long videos. This happens because regressing poses relative to a fixed first-frame anchor forces extrapolation far beyond the training distribution. Small drifts accumulate and amplify into significant geometric collapse. However, we observe that per-frame depth remains stable throughout this failure. The backbone's local geometry remains intact; only the global pose head breaks down. Motivated by this decoupling, we introduce Scal3R. This approach reformulates online reconstruction as multi-reference relative pose querying. We use lightweight learnable tokens, which make up about ~1% of the parameters, and inject them into a completely frozen backbone via asymmetric attention. This setup queries poses relative to multiple past keyframes. An online pose-graph optimization system with loop closure suppresses long-range drift. Scal3R reaches convergence in 8 hours on a single GPU. It reduces the average ATE by over 60% on KITTI compared to the online baseline. It also achieves state-of-the-art performance across Virtual KITTI, Sintel, TUM-Dynamic, ScanNet, and 7-Scenes. Project page: https://linjohnss.github.io/scal3r/

</details>


### [101] [TokenMatch: 3D Mesh Correspondence Transformer with Curvature-Guided Tokenisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04202)
*Adeela Islam,Zorah Lähner,Vittorio Murino,Vladislav Golyanik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TokenMatch是一个基于Transformer的统一模型，用于3D形状对应估计。它仅在BeCoS（一个具有挑战性的非等距部分到部分形状匹配数据集）上训练，无需再训练或微调即可泛化到完整形状匹配。模型利用自注意力和交叉注意力机制学习点级、补丁级关系及稠密对应，其核心创新在于根据曲率引导将网格自适应地标记化为补丁，从而学习形状特定的几何描述符。在多个标准基准（包括CP2P、PSMAL、BeCoS、FAUST、SCAPE、SHREC'19）上，该方法在平均测地误差和交并比指标上取得了一致的高性能，多数情况下优于现有方法，且推理速度快于亚秒级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的方法通常依赖手工设计的描述符或基于模板的表征，而近期基于功能映射的生成模型存在推理成本高、可解释性有限以及难以泛化到部分形状等问题。部分观测和非等距变形下的鲁棒形状匹配仍然是一个巨大挑战，因此需要一种新的统一方法来同时应对部分和完整形状匹配。

Method: TokenMatch采用前馈Transformer架构：利用自注意力和交叉注意力机制高效学习形状对之间的补丁级和点级关系及稠密对应。其核心洞察是基于曲率引导将网格自适应地标记化为补丁，使模型能够有效地学习形状特定的几何描述符用于对应估计。该方法仅在全新构建的BeCoS数据集上训练，无需对完整形状匹配进行额外训练或微调。

Result: 在包括CP2P、PSMAL、BeCoS、FAUST、SCAPE和SHREC'19在内的多个标准基准上进行评估，TokenMatch在平均测地误差和交并比等指标上取得了一致的高性能，在大多数情况下优于现有的部分和完整形状匹配方法，同时推理速度更快，达到亚秒级。

Conclusion: TokenMatch证明了仅在一个挑战性的非等距部分到部分匹配数据集上训练的统一前馈Transformer模型，能够有效泛化到完整形状匹配任务，同时兼具高性能和高效推理的优势，为局部到完整形状泛化提供了新的范式。

Abstract: While data-driven 3D shape correspondence estimation has recently seen substantial progress, robust matching under partial observations and strong non-isometric deformations remains challenging. Existing learning-based approaches often rely on hand-crafted descriptors or template-based representations, whereas recent generative models over functional maps suffer from high inference cost, limited interpretability, and poor generalisation to partial shapes. In response to these limitations, this paper introduces TokenMatch, a new transformer-based unified model for estimating 3D shape correspondences. Our feed-forward approach trained exclusively on BeCoS, a challenging non-isometric partial-to-partial shape-matching dataset, can generalise to matching full shapes without retraining or fine-tuning. TokenMatch uses self- and cross-attention mechanisms to efficiently learn patch-level and point-level relations as well as dense correspondences between shape pairs. Our core insight is that meshes can be adaptively tokenised into patches using shape curvature guidance, enabling effective learning of shape-specific geometric descriptors for correspondence estimation. We evaluate TokenMatch on standard benchmarks for partial and full shape matching, including CP2P, PSMAL, BeCoS, FAUST, SCAPE, and SHREC'19. Our method achieves consistently high performance, in most cases outperforming existing methods for partial and full shape matching in the mean geodesic error and intersection-over-union metrics, while also running faster at sub-second inference speeds.

</details>


### [102] [Temporal Self-Distillation: Learning Visual State Tracking in Videos Without Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04203)
*Shravan Venkatraman,Wenshuai Zhao,Mohammad Hassan Vali,Arno Solin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出S^3T框架，利用时间采样密度作为特权信息，通过密集视图引导稀疏视图进行自监督自蒸馏，无需标签、独立教师或奖励信号即可实现连续视频状态跟踪，在多个基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续视频状态跟踪是重要的视频理解任务，但现有方法通常依赖人工标签、独立教师模型或额外奖励信号，成本高昂且扩展性差。作者假设同一片段中更密集的时间采样视图能更准确地恢复运行状态，希望利用这一"特权信息"构建一个完全自包含的训练框架，摆脱对外部监督的依赖。

Method: S^3T（Self-Supervised Self-Distillation over Time）将时间采样密度视为特权信息：密集视图作为教师，稀疏视图作为学生（两者共享相同权重），学生需匹配教师的下一个token分布。模型自己生成训练目标，无需标签、独立教师或奖励信号，不增加推理成本。

Result: 在LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B上，S^3T使VSTAT准确率单模型提升+1.74，模型融合（souping）+2.38，额外的视觉编码器适配+2.70，而先前自进化方法对状态跟踪几乎没有改善。从无标签合成片段学到的能力可迁移到真实视频：VSTAT-YouTube状态跟踪问题提升+7.95，MVBench Action Count提升+4.50。

Conclusion: S^3T是首个完全自包含的连续视频状态跟踪框架，验证了"时间采样密度作为特权信息"这一假设的有效性，并通过自蒸馏实现无需外部监督的持续改进，同时在合成到真实的迁移中展现出良好泛化能力。

Abstract: We introduce S$^3$T (Self-Supervised Self-Distillation over Time), which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first fully self-contained framework for continuous video state tracking. Our method treats temporal sampling density as privileged information, based on the hypothesis that a denser view of the same clip recovers the running state more accurately. This view serves as the teacher, while a sparse-view student with the same weights learns to match its next-token distribution. The model generates its own target, so training requires no labels, separate teacher, or reward signal, and adds no inference cost. On LLaVA-OneVision-2-8B, S$^3$T improves VSTAT accuracy by $+1.74$ as a single model, $+2.38$ with souping, and $+2.70$ with additional vision-encoder adaptation, while prior self-evolving methods leave state tracking largely unchanged. The capability learned from unlabeled synthetic clips transfers to real videos, improving performance by $+7.95$ on VSTAT-YouTube state-tracking questions and $+4.50$ on MVBench Action Count.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Learning from Scarce Labels: Multi-View Echocardiography for Ejection Fraction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02969)
*Zhiyuan Gao,Dominic Yurk,Yaser S. Abu-Mostafa*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 作者构建了首个公开可用的胸骨旁长轴(PLAX)切面超声心动图左室射血分数(EF)预测资源。通过利用临床记录与超声视频之间的时间相关性、微调视图分类器和代理标签等创新数据生成策略，创建了超过25,000个PLAX视频的标注数据集，并训练了首个可复现的PLAX EF模型，MAE达6.86%，与临床标准的A4C方法(6%-7%)相当。将PLAX与A4C预测进行简单无权重晚期融合可将MAE进一步降至6.37%。数据集标签、模型及演示均已开源发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前不存在任何PLAX-EF公开数据集，而胸骨旁长轴切面在某些临床场景(如心尖切面不可获取)下对EF评估具有重要价值。现有方法均基于心尖四腔(A4C)视角，缺乏对PLAX视角EF可行性的系统验证和开源资源。

Method: (1) 提出创新的数据生成策略：利用临床记录与超声视频之间的时间相关性进行代理标签(proxy labeling)，结合微调视图分类器筛选PLAX视频；(2) 构建包含超过25,000个PLAX视频的标注数据集；(3) 训练首个可复现的PLAX EF预测模型；(4) 通过将PLAX与A4C预测进行简单的无权重晚期融合实现多视角信息整合。

Result: PLAX EF模型达到6.86%的MAE，与临床标准A4C方法报出的6%-7% MAE相当甚至更优，超过现有方法表现。PLAX与A4C的晚期融合进一步将MAE降低至6.37%，优于任一单视角基线。

Conclusion: PLAX切面进行EF估计在临床上是可行且相关的，为心尖切面不可用的临床情况提供了有效解决方案。多视角信息融合能进一步提升EF预测精度。所有资源已公开于GitHub、Hugging Face和Google Colab，以促进后续研究。

Abstract: We present, to the best of our knowledge, the first publicly available resource for predicting left ventricular ejection fraction (EF) from parasternal long-axis (PLAX) echocardiography. Because no PLAX-EF datasets previously existed, our work focuses on an innovative data generation strategy to overcome this scarcity. By leveraging a time-based correlation between clinical notes and echocardiographic videos, combined with fine-tuning view classifiers and proxy labeling, we created a labeled dataset of over 25,000 PLAX videos. This enables us to train the first reproducible PLAX EF model, achieving a mean absolute error (MAE) of 6.86%. Given that apical four-chamber (A4C) methods, the clinical standard, report MAE values of 6%-7%, our results demonstrate that EF estimation from PLAX views is both feasible and clinically relevant. This surpasses the performance of existing methods and provides a clinically relevant solution for situations where apical views may not be feasible. Going further, we demonstrate that combining PLAX and A4C predictions via simple unweighted late fusion improves both single-view baselines to a 6.37% MAE, underscoring the value of multi-view integration. To promote continued research, we release the dataset labels, trained models, and runnable demos on GitHub, Hugging Face, and Google Colab: https://github.com/Jeffrey4899/PLAX_EF_Labels_202509

</details>


### [104] [Beyond Blur: A Semantic Tri-view Pipeline for Teledermatology Gradability via Skin Micro-relief](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03095)
*Robert Engel*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出语义三视图流水线（Semantic Tri-view Pipeline），一种可解释的自动化远程皮肤病学可分性（gradability）筛查架构。它利用轻量级DeepLabV3+模型对表皮微浮雕（epidermal micro-relief）进行分割，作为图像质量的可计算生物标志物，然后通过逻辑回归分类器跨最多三个病例视图聚合空间掩码。在SCIN数据集上，该系统在明显无歧义病例上AUC从0.81提升至0.96（PPV从80.6%升至97.7%），提供隐私保护、边缘可部署的实时反馈能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机皮肤照片对远程皮肤病学至关重要，但自动评估提交病例的诊断适用性（可分性）仍是移动护理工作流的关键瓶颈。皮肤科医生通常需要查看多个摄影视图来识别一致性的纹理细节，而现有方法缺乏一个可解释、能利用多视图冗余的自动筛查方案。

Method: 首先，使用专家注释的SCIN公共数据集子集训练轻量级DeepLabV3+模型来分割表皮微浮雕保真度（micro-relief fidelity），将表皮微浮雕形式化为图像质量的可计算生物标志物。然后，将最多三个病例视图（区域、角度和特写）的空间分割掩码聚合到逻辑回归分类器中，利用视点冗余增强在非受控智能手机采集下的鲁棒性。该方法能学习上下文感知、临床可理解的启发式规则，如在区域远景视图中惩罚高保真纹理。

Result: 在预设90%灵敏度工作点下，系统表现出的误差主要反映边界病例上依赖非视觉元数据的主观临床差异。在SCIN数据集上，性能从方差较大的多数共识病例的AUC 0.81（PPV 80.6%）提升到视觉无歧义一致病例的AUC 0.96（PPV 97.7%）。

Conclusion: 该系统提供了可解释、隐私保护、边缘可部署（edge-ready）的解决方案，可在病例提交期间提供实时反馈，在医生审阅前过滤掉不可分级的照片集，从而有效解决远程皮肤病学工作流中的可分性瓶颈。

Abstract: Smartphone skin photographs are indispensable to teledermatology, yet assessing the diagnostic suitability of submitted cases (gradability) remains a critical bottleneck in mobile care workflows. Dermatologists routinely review multiple photographic views (regional, angled, and close-up) to identify consistent textural detail rather than relying on a single image. We present the Semantic Tri-view Pipeline, an interpretable architecture for automated teledermatology gradability screening that formalizes epidermal micro-relief as a computable biomarker of image quality. Using an expert-annotated subset of the public SCIN dataset, we train a lightweight DeepLabV3+ model to segment micro-relief fidelity. These spatial masks are then aggregated across up to three case views with a logistic regression classifier, leveraging viewpoint redundancy to support robustness under uncontrolled smartphone acquisition. This approach learns context-aware, clinically intelligible heuristics, such as penalizing high-fidelity texture in regional distance views. Evaluated at a predefined 90% sensitivity operating point, the system's apparent errors largely reflect subjective clinical variance on borderline cases where clinicians rely on non-visual metadata. On SCIN, performance improves from an AUC of 0.81 (80.6% PPV) on variance-heavy majority-consensus cases to 0.96 (97.7% PPV) on optically unambiguous unanimous cases. Overall, this work delivers an interpretable, privacy-by-design, edge-ready system that can provide real-time feedback during case submission to filter ungradable photo sets before review.

</details>


### [105] [Plant Growth Estimation with a Camera-Based Vegetation Index Mapping System for Agricultural Ground Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03872)
*Lukas Pindl,Michael Maier,Timo Oksanen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于相机和RTK-GNSS的农业田间作物生长测绘传感器，通过透视投影将多光谱图像投影到地面平面并计算NDVI，可在低端硬件上实时处理图像。与传统商用传感器相比趋势一致，并支持过滤非地面区域以减少作物密度对NDVI均值的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种低成本、可实时处理且安装在车载平台上（如拖拉机）的作物生长测绘传感器，以替代或补充价格昂贵、较难比较绝对值的商用传感器。

Method: 使用相机采集多光谱图像，结合RTK-GNSS定位将图像按透视投影映射到估计的地面平面，从而拼接成田间地图；以NDVI作为植物生长估计指标，支持其他指标；可对地面与非地面区域进行滤波。

Result: 与商用车辆载传感器相比，绝对数值难以直接比较，但两者测得的趋势一致；相机方案支持低端硬件下的实时处理，并可通过地面/非地面滤波减少作物密度对平均NDVI的干扰。

Conclusion: 该相机式传感器提供了一种计算简单、实时性强的作物生长测绘方案，能有效匹配商用传感器的趋势表现，并具备额外过滤能力以提升NDVI测量的可靠性。

Abstract: This work presents a camera-based sensor for mapping plant growth over an agricultural field. The sensor can be used on ground-based vehicles like a tractor and relies on RTK-GNSS to correctly merge many multispectral images onto one map. NDVI is used as an index to estimate plant growth, but the approach can be used with other indices as well depending on the camera. The images from the camera are projected onto the estimated ground plane using perspective projection. This computationally simple approach allows for real-time processing of all images on the field even with low-end hardware. The results are compared to a commercially established vehicle-mounted sensor. Absolute values are hard to compare, but both sensors show similar trends. The camera-based approach also allows for filtering of ground and non-ground areas, potentially reducing the impact of crop density on the average measured NDVI.

</details>


### [106] [Computationally Efficient Pathology Segmentation using Knowledge Distillation from Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03947)
*Jiaqi Lv,Yijie Zhu,Saki Okada,Maxwell Stanley Renna,Robert Goldin,Stefan Antonowicz,Shan E Ahmed Raza*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于基础模型知识蒸馏的框架，用于高效的组织病理学组织分割。研究者首先训练基于Virchow2的分割教师模型（含LoRA变体），在PUMA、IGNITE、BEETTE及私有血管分割数据集上取得最先进或极具竞争力的性能；随后通过响应级和特征级蒸馏将这些知识迁移到紧凑的学生网络中。蒸馏一致性地优于纯监督训练，在参数大幅减少且推理吞吐量提升最高达十倍的情况下仍保持最先进或接近最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织病理学全切片图像（WSI）的大规模分析需要自动组织分割，但像素级标注成本高昂，自然图像预训练模型迁移到组织病理学表现不佳，而病理基础模型虽表征能力强却计算开销大，难以大规模部署。

Method: 采用基础模型知识蒸馏框架：首先基于Virchow2训练分割教师模型（包括LoRA适配变体），然后通过响应级（response-level）和特征级（feature-level）蒸馏将教师知识迁移到紧凑的学生网络中，在四个数据集上进行评估。

Result: 教师模型在PUMA、IGNITE、BEETLE及私有血管数据集上达到最先进或极具竞争力的性能；蒸馏一致性地优于纯监督训练，学生模型参数量大幅减少，推理吞吐量比基础模型分割网络高至10倍，同时保持最先进或接近最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 研究表明基础模型知识可以有效迁移至高效的分割模型，在不牺牲性能的前提下实现可扩展部署。公共数据集上训练的模型将通过TIAToolbox发布。

Abstract: Automatic tissue segmentation is essential for large-scale analysis of histopathology whole-slide images (WSIs), but accurate pixel-level segmentation remains challenging. Pixel-level annotations are expensive to obtain, models pre-trained on natural images may transfer poorly to histopathology, and pathology foundation models, despite their strong representations, are computationally expensive to deploy at scale. We address these challenges with a foundation-model knowledge distillation framework for efficient tissue segmentation. We first train Virchow2-based segmentation teachers, including a LoRA-adapted variant, achieving state-of-the-art or highly competitive performance across four datasets: PUMA, IGNITE, BEETLE, and a private blood vessel segmentation dataset. We then transfer response-level and feature-level knowledge from these teachers into compact student networks. Distillation consistently improves student performance over supervised training alone, producing state-of-the-art or near state-of-the-art results with substantially fewer parameters and up to ten-fold higher inference throughput than foundation-model-based segmentation networks. These results show that foundation-model knowledge can be effectively transferred to efficient segmentation models for scalable deployment without compromising performance. Models trained on the public datasets will be released through TIAToolbox.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [Structure and Implementation of New Practical English Textbooks Driven by Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02981)
*Ya Wang,Lei Zhang,Xueguang Yang,Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并测试了一种由人工智能驱动的应用英语教材系统，其采用五层架构（知识图谱、学习者画像、任务生成、反馈编排、教师端治理）。在186名非英语专业本科生中进行了八周教学实验，结果显示该系统相比静态数字教材将单元完成准确率从72.4%提升至84.9%，口语任务平均分提高10.8分，教师批改时间减少31.6%，表明AI驱动教材能在保持课程稳定性的同时提供个性化学习路径和可追踪的课堂数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能正在将应用英语教材从固定纸质序列转变为能够诊断学习者、推荐任务并提供形成性反馈的适应性学习系统，但目前对这一新型教材的结构和应用研究不足，因此需要探索AI驱动英语教材的系统架构及其实践效果。

Method: 提出一个五层架构的AI驱动应用英语教材系统，包括：知识图谱层、学习者画像层、任务生成层、反馈编排层和教师端治理层。并构建原型系统，对186名非英语专业本科生进行为期八周的教学对照实验，与静态数字教材进行效果比较。

Result: 实验结果显示，与静态数字教材相比，AI驱动系统将单元完成准确率从72.4%提升至84.9%，口语任务平均分提升了10.8分，教师批改时间减少了31.6%。

Conclusion: AI驱动的教材能够在维持课程体系稳定性的前提下，提供个性化学习路径、丰富的练习材料和可追踪的课堂数据，是一种有效的新型教材形态。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is changing the form of applied English materials from fixed paper sequences to adaptive learning systems that can diagnose learners, recommend tasks, and provide formative feedback. This paper studies the structure and application of a new practical English textbook driven by artificial intelligence. A five-layer architecture is proposed: knowledge mapping, learner profiling, task generation, feedback orchestration, and teacher-side governance. A prototype was tested on 186 non-English-major undergraduates for eight weeks of teaching. Compared with a static digital textbook, the proposed system increased the unit completion accuracy from 72.4% to 84.9%, raised the average score for speaking tasks by 10.8 points, and reduced the teacher's correction time by 31.6%. Therefore, an AI-driven textbook can maintain the stability of the curriculum while providing personalised learning paths, rich practice materials and traceable classroom data.

</details>


### [108] [MasterControl Seventeen Every Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03209)
*MasterControl AI Lab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种受治理（governed approach）的企业分析框架：语言模型仅负责解释意图，而确定性策略负责选择并执行预先审批的分析程序，该程序同时返回结果与证据。通过在440次运行中比较三种8B模型（三个模型在运行时生成SQL并选择工具，而Qwen3-8B仅解释意图并由策略执行已批准的程程序），结果显示：330次运行时规划（runtime planning）均未能完全满足"答案+证据"契约，而策略执行的解析器在110/110次全部匹配成功。作者指出这是特定配置下的结果，不能据此断言运行时智能体在其他设计下无法成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业分析需要在表达能力（expressiveness）与治理合规（governance compliance）之间取得平衡。现有的运行时生成计划（runtime planning）方案虽灵活，但难以保证执行的可控性与结果的证据可追溯性。本文旨在验证一种更受约束的架构——让语言模型只负责意图理解、由确定性策略执行预批准的"固定程序"——是否既能保持足够的表达范围，又能确保结果可重现（replayable）且满足结果+证据的完整输出契约。

Method: 采用"语言模型解释问题 + 确定性策略选择并运行预批准分析程序"的分离架构。分析程序限定在由关系运算、聚合、比较、窗口函数、排序和相似度组成的分析类别内。通过固定语义、策略、数据与执行规则来保证输出可重放。实验使用三个8B模型在运行时生成SQL并自主选择工具（共330次运行时规划），另用Qwen3-8B仅做意图解释、由策略执行已批准程序（110次），对全部440次运行做对照。

Result: 330次运行时规划场景无一在所有测试数据集上完整满足"答案加证据"契约；而由策略执行预批准程序的方案在110/110次（100%）全部匹配成功。结果表明受治理的固定程序执行在满足完整输出契约方面显著优于当前配置下的运行时自由规划。

Conclusion: 本文证明，在企业分析这一限定领域内，将生成能力约束在意图层、由确定性策略执行预批准程序，既保持了覆盖指定分析类别（关系运算、聚合、窗口、排序、相似度等）的表达力，又能实现100%的结果与证据一致性输出。但该结论是配置特定的，不应过度泛化为否定运行时智能体在其他设计下的可行性。

Abstract: We study a governed approach to enterprise analytics: a language model interprets the question, while deterministic policy selects and runs a pre-approved analytical program that returns both results and evidence. We show that this restriction can remain expressive within a defined analytical class, using relational operations plus aggregation, comparison, windows, ranking, and similarity. Fixed meaning, policy, data, and execution rules also make results replayable. Across 440 runs, three 8B models generated SQL and selected tools at runtime, while Qwen3-8B interpreted intent only and policy executed the approved program. None of 330 runtime-planning episodes matched the full answer-and-evidence contract across all test datasets; the policy-executed analyzer matched 110 of 110. This is a configuration-specific result, not evidence that runtime agents cannot succeed under other designs.

</details>


### [109] [Speculative Macro Commit for Faster Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03236)
*Zeyu Liu,Souvik Kundu,Peter A. Beerel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SMC是一种用于双层智能体系统的运行时机制，通过从训练轨迹中挖掘重复的多动作骨架存入宏库，利用快速推测模型预执行动作链，当权威模型的工具调用与预测首步匹配时，将预执行步骤及观察提交到官方轨迹，从而实现多步推测执行，降低智能体延迟。在τ²-Bench Telecom和AppWorld上分别较顺序执行降低18.59%和44.9%的延迟，同时保持总体准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具型LLM智能体在串行执行过程中，每次工具调用、环境转换和观察都会引入大量墙钟时间延迟，不仅消耗在模型推理上，还消耗在串行的动作-观察回合中。现有单步推测动作（Speculative Actions）方法仅支持单步加速，无法充分利用多步预测来进一步降低延迟，因此需要一种能在保持准确率的同时实现多步推测执行和复用的机制。

Method: SMC采用双层智能体架构：大型权威演员模型（Qwen3.5-27B INT4）生成官方轨迹，快速的推测草稿模型（Qwen3.5-4B）持续预测并隔离环境快照上执行未来动作链。具体流程包括：(1) 从训练轨迹中挖掘重复出现的多动作骨架存入宏库；(2) 运行时将草稿模型预测的动作链与宏库中的宏进行匹配；(3) 当演员模型的下一步工具调用与草稿模型预测的首个动作匹配时，SMC将剩余的预执行草稿步骤及其观察一并提交到官方轨迹中。

Result: 在τ²-Bench Telecom子集上，SMC与顺序执行相比保持总体准确率一致，同时延迟比Speculative Actions（SA）基线上限降低10.23%，比顺序执行降低18.59%。在AppWorld上，SMC相比SA基线降低7.7%的墙钟时间，比顺序执行降低44.9%，但任务完成率有轻微下降。代码已公开。

Conclusion: SMC提供了一种实用的多步推测执行复用方案，超越单步推测动作的加速效果，有效降低工具型LLM智能体的运行延迟，同时在不同基准上基本保持任务性能。该方法展示了从训练数据中挖掘可复用宏动作模式来加速智能体执行的有效性。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents spend wall-clock time not only on model inference but also in serial action--observation turns, where each tool call, environment transition, and observation can delay subsequent decisions. We introduce \textbf{Speculative Macro Commit} (SMC), a runtime mechanism for a two-tier agent system: a large authoritative actor model produces the official trajectory, while a faster speculative drafter model continuously predicts and executes future action chains on an isolated environment snapshot. SMC mines recurring multi-action skeletons from training traces and stores them in a macro library used to match against action chains predicted by the drafter at runtime. When the actor's next tool call matches the first drafted action, SMC commits the remaining pre-executed draft steps, together with their observations, to the official trajectory. Using Qwen3.5-27B INT4 as the authoritative actor model and Qwen3.5-4B as the speculative drafter model, SMC matches the sequential agent's overall accuracy while reducing latency by 10.23\% over the Speculative Actions (SA) baseline and 18.59\% over sequential execution on the $τ^2$-Bench Telecom subset. On AppWorld, SMC reduces wall time by 7.7\% over SA baseline and 44.9\% over sequential execution, with a small reduction in task completion. Overall, SMC provides a practical way to reuse multi-step speculative execution and reduce agent latency beyond single-step speculative actions. Our code is publicly available \href{https://github.com/zeyuliu1037/speculative-macro-commit}{\textcolor{magenta}{here}}.

</details>


### [110] [Fresh Memory, Stale Plans: Dependency-Scoped Validation for Distributed LLM-Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03340)
*Evan Chen,Shiqiang Wang,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PlanFence协议，解决分布式LLM智能体团队中的"过期计划执行"（stale-plan execution）问题：即共享状态已更新但计划未同步失效的情况。PlanFence通过让计划引用其使用的精确公共记录，执行器仅验证可能影响待执行外部动作的记录，必要时重新规划或阻塞。在30个受控实时工作流中，PlanFence全部任务无无效动作完成，而仅依赖状态新鲜度的执行器在每项任务中都执行了过期计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作假设"状态新鲜度足以保证行动计划有效"，但即使分布式LLM智能体团队读取了最新共享事实，仍可能基于过期计划行动——计划者可能基于r3推导动作，其他智能体提交r4，而执行器收到r4却未替换基于r3的计划。这种状态新鲜与计划有效性之间的鸿沟未被现有系统解决。

Method: PlanFence是一种依赖范围的行动验证协议，包含两个核心机制：(1) 计划明确引用其生成时使用的精确公共记录（plan引用机制）；(2) 执行器在外部动作执行前，仅验证可能影响该待执行动作的相关记录，验证不完整时触发一次性重新规划或阻塞。

Result: 在30个受控实时工作流（含计划后修订）中，仅依赖新鲜度的执行器在每项任务中均执行过期计划，而PlanFence在所有任务中完成且无无效动作。受控回放揭示两个边界：(1) 低变更率下，主动同步产生更低的协调停滞；(2) 随着变更率增长，PlanFence避免了重复的更新路径协调；随共享键空间增长，PlanFence避免验证无关状态。

Conclusion: PlanFence提供了受控的安全性和系统成本结果——能够消除过期计划执行，代价是在低变更率场景下比主动同步有更高的协调停滞。这些是安全性和系统成本方面的受控结果，并非通用任务准确率的提升。

Abstract: Distributed LLM-agent teams can read the latest shared facts and still act on an obsolete plan. A planner may derive an action from requirement $r_3$, another agent may commit $r_4$, and an executor may receive $r_4$ without replacing the plan derived from $r_3$. We call this \emph{stale-plan execution}: state freshness does not establish that the plan authorizing an action remains valid. We introduce PlanFence, a dependency-scoped action-validation protocol. Plans cite the exact public records they used, and an executor validates only the records that can affect the pending external action, replanning once or blocking when validation is incomplete. In 30 controlled live workflows with a post-plan revision, a freshness-only executor acts on the obsolete plan in every task, whereas PlanFence completes all tasks without an invalid action. Controlled replay reveals two conditional boundaries: proactive synchronization yields lower coordination stall at low churn, while PlanFence avoids repeated update-path coordination as churn grows and avoids validating unrelated state as the shared keyspace grows. These are controlled safety and systems-cost results, not general task-accuracy gains.

</details>


### [111] [A Prompt-Engineering Approach to Develop Scalable, Flexible, and Real-Time Hybrid Micro-Level Personalization in a General Purpose AI Teaching Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03402)
*Saptarshi Basu,Sandeep Kakar,Ashok Goel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于提示工程（Prompt Engineering）的框架，用于个性化定制基于大语言模型（LLM）和RAG的AI教学助手（如Jill Watson），使其能够跨学科和课程提供适应性教育支持。框架利用六个学习者维度（自我评估、抽象偏好、冗长偏好、知觉取向、信息处理风格和理解水平）组合出96种学习者画像，并结合Bloom分类法对查询进行认知复杂度分析，将所得信息编码为结构化提示以条件化LLM输出，无需模型重训练。实验结果（NLP指标与5名参与者的人工研究）表明不同个性化条件下的响应在风格和结构上存在可感知差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的AI教学助手虽然能够提供可扩展的教育支持，但个性化能力有限。研究者希望在不依赖模型微调或重训练的前提下，通过提示工程实现针对学习者个体差异（认知风格、理解水平等）的自适应教学响应，使通用型LLM/RAG教育智能体能够跨学科和课程提供个性化教育支持。

Method: 该研究提出一个基于提示工程的个性化框架：（1）定义六个学习者特定维度（自我评估、抽象偏好、冗长偏好、知觉取向、信息处理风格和理解水平），组合生成96种学习者画像；（2）使用Bloom分类法在交互层面对学生查询进行认知复杂度估计；（3）将学习者属性和认知评估编码为结构化提示，条件化LLM输出而无需模型重训练；（4）通过NLP自动评估指标和包含5名参与者的人工研究进行实验验证，并用统计分析识别与可测量响应变化相关的最显著学习者属性。

Result: 实验结果揭示了不同个性化条件下响应风格和结构上的感知差异；统计分析了识别出与可测量响应变化相关联的学习者属性。这些发现为基于提示的个性化能够在LLM驱动的教育智能体中支持自适应行为提供了初步证据。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过结构化的提示工程编码学习者多维画像与Bloom认知层级，可以实现无需重训练的LLM教育助手个性化适配，为开发真正具有自适应能力的AI教学助手指明了可行路径。然而，由于人工研究样本量较小（仅5名参与者），当前证据仍属初步性质，需要更大规模的实证研究加以验证。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) teaching assistants powered by large language models (LLMs) offer scalable educational support but often provide limited personalization. This study presents a prompt-engineering-based framework for personalizing general-purpose LLM/RAG-based AI teaching assistants such as Jill Watson across academic disciplines and courses. The framework adapts responses using six learner-specific dimensions: self-assessment, abstraction preference, verbosity preference, perceptual orientation, information processing style, and level of understanding, yielding 96 distinct learner profiles. Student queries are additionally analyzed using Bloom's Taxonomy to estimate cognitive complexity at the interaction level. Learner attributes and cognitive assessments are encoded in structured prompts that condition the LLM without requiring model retraining. The framework is evaluated through experiments using NLP metrics and a human study with five participants. Results show perceived differences in response style and structure across personalization conditions, with statistical analyses identifying learner attributes associated with measurable response changes. These findings provide preliminary evidence that prompt-based personalization can support adaptive behavior in LLM-powered educational agents.

</details>


### [112] [Caught in the Story: Narrative Captivity in Multi-turn LLMs Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03407)
*Yuhe Wu,Guangyu Wang,Yujie Chen,Jiatong Zhang,Yuran Chen,Yutong Zhang,Xiyin Cheng,Wenpeng Cao,Zhuang Liu,Guang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"叙事俘获"（narrative captivity）这一失效模式：在多轮道德咨询中，大型语言模型会将未经驳斥的单方面叙事视为完整信息，从而放弃独立判断并认同叙述者的立场。作者构建了包含5,078个六维人际冲突场景的基准测试，在17个LLM上验证该现象普遍存在：多轮叙事的最终判断相比单轮基线平均偏移高达25个百分点。逐阶段分析表明偏好优化是主要诱因，四种推理期策略仅能部分缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们越来越多地依赖LLM获取日常道德建议，但既有研究多基于单轮判断或施压式反驳的设定，不符合真实咨询场景。真实世界中的道德冲突往往以一方自我辩护的叙事为形式，在多轮对话中逐步展开并形成信息不对称。现有研究未回答：仅靠单方面叙述（无需明确对立立场）是否足以在多轮道德咨询中改变模型判断。

Method: 提出"叙事俘获"概念；构建包含5,078个人际冲突场景的基准，涵盖六个道德维度；在17个LLM上进行系统评测，对比多轮叙事与匹配单轮基线的最终判断差异；通过阶段级分析定位偏好优化（preference optimization）的贡献；评估四种推理期缓解策略。

Result: 叙事俘获在17个LLM中普遍存在：多轮叙事的最终判断相对单轮基线平均偏移25个百分点；阶段级分析表明偏好优化是导致该现象的主要因素；四种推理期策略（如推理提示等）只能提供部分缓解效果。

Conclusion: 叙事俘获是多轮道德咨询中的系统性风险，LLM顾问需保持独立判断能力。研究呼吁开发能够识别信息缺失、主动寻求多视角的LLM道德顾问，以更好服务于真实世界咨询场景。

Abstract: People increasingly turn to large language models (LLMs) for everyday advice, making ethically charged interpersonal problems a practical moral-advisory context. Most prior work has studied this context through single-turn judgments or pressure-laden rebuttals, assumptions that poorly match how guidance is sought in real-world contexts. These assumptions leave unclear whether narration alone, without an explicit opposing position, can shift model judgments during multi-turn moral consultation. Yet real-world moral-conflict conversation often elicits one party's self-justifying account, which can unfold over multiple turns and create information asymmetry. We introduce \textbf{narrative captivity}, a failure mode in which a model treats an unopposed one-sided account as complete and aligns with the narrator's interpretation without seeking missing perspectives. To measure this phenomenon, we build a benchmark of $5{,}078$ interpersonal-conflict scenarios spanning six moral dimensions. Across 17 LLMs, narrative captivity is widespread: end-state judgments under multi-turn narration shift by 25 percentage points on average beyond the matched single-turn baseline. Stage-level analysis identifies preference optimization as a major contributor, while four inference-time strategies provide only partial mitigation. We hope our project fosters LLM advisors that preserve independent judgment in real-world consultation.

</details>


### [113] [Dude: A Dual-Detection Multi-Agent System for Paper-Code Discrepancy Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03416)
*Weijie Liu,Running Zhao,Wenhao Yuan,Jinfeng Xu,Zhanfeng Xu,Xiaoxi Zhang,Edith Cheuk-Han Ngai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Dude，首个用于论文-代码不一致检测的双检测多智能体系统，通过粒度对齐协商和两阶段显著性过滤机制，解决了现有单智能体范式因上下文有限和检测片面导致的低召回率问题，以及多智能体设计中粒度不对称带来的误报问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研论文提交数量激增，超过人工审阅能力，LLM赋能的论文-代码不一致检测受到关注。然而现有单智能体LLM范式存在上下文容量有限和检测片面性问题，导致检测不一致时召回率表现较差。

Method: 提出Dude——首个双检测多智能体系统。针对论文语言与代码语言间粒度不对称导致的过度解读和过度报告问题，设计了粒度对齐协商机制和两阶段显著性过滤机制，防止智能体虚假报告不一致。

Result: 在真实世界论文-代码不一致数据集上，Dude相比基线方法召回率和精确率最高提升22.8%，F1分数最高提升18.7%。

Conclusion: Dude通过多智能体双检测架构及粒度对齐与显著性过滤机制，有效提升了论文-代码不一致检测的准确性和召回率，验证了该方法在真实数据集上的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-empowered paper-code discrepancy detection has received growing concern since the scaling of research submissions exceeds the manual review capability. However, the limited context capacity and one-sided discrepancy detection of existing single-agent LLM paradigms lead to an inferior recall performance in detecting discrepancies. In this paper, we propose Dude, the first Dual-Detection Multi-Agent System for paper-code discrepancy detection. We discover that the granularity asymmetry of the paper-language and code-language introduces over-interpretation and over-reporting challenges in a multi-agent system design for discrepancy detection, resulting in increasing false positives. To address this, we propose a granularity-aligned negotiation and a two-stage salience-filtering mechanism in Dude, which effectively prevents agents from falsely reporting discrepancies. Experimental results in real-world paper-code discrepancy datasets showcase Dude's significant recall and precision improvement by up to 22.8%, increasing F1 score by up to 18.7% compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [114] [DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval: Evaluating Implicit Instruction Following in Full-Duplex Voice Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03423)
*Puneet Mathur,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval（DSB-IFEval），一个用于评估全双工语音智能体在实时口语交互中隐式指令遵循能力的基准。该基准包含1,038个测试用例，覆盖八种不同的助手角色和五种指令调节协议。研究在六个实时语音系统上进行评估，发现不同架构在显式与隐式（由人格角色推断）指令遵循上存在权衡，且系统在安全冲突下难以覆盖人物设定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准大多通过显式话轮管理指令来测试语音智能体的对话行为，但实际部署的智能体往往通过角色或人格而非显式指令来配置，需要从中推断合适的对话行为。缺乏评估这种隐式指令遵循能力的基准。

Method: 构建DSB-IFEval基准，包含1,038个测试用例和八个助手角色；评估五种指令调节协议（默认行为、显式行为指令、人格隐含行为、人格-规则组合调节、指令冲突）；采用确定性的指令遵循得分（IAS）衡量实时话轮管理，用LLM评判的人格一致性得分（PAS）衡量内容一致性，在六个实时语音系统上进行评估。

Result: 发现架构相关的权衡：全双工模型（F-Actor、PersonaPlex）对行为是否显式陈述或需从人格推断较敏感，在仅人格条件下遵循度分别下降9.7%和4.5%；而GPT-Realtime、MiniCPM-o、Fun-Audio-Chat能强遵循人格一致内容，但其话轮管理行为在不同调节协议间不调整且在前瞻性动作上受限。此外，即使在指令冲突下系统能可靠遵循预设人格，仍难以在安全冲突时覆盖这些指令。

Conclusion: 推断角色所隐含的行为、在恰当对话时机执行该行为、以及解决竞争性指令，对全双工语音智能体而言是三个截然不同的挑战。

Abstract: Full-duplex voice agents must continuously decide when to listen, backchannel, interrupt, handle speech overlaps, take the floor, and yield. Existing benchmarks largely test these behaviors through explicit turn-management instructions, while deployed agents are often configured through roles or personas from which the appropriate conversational behavior must be inferred. We introduce DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval (DSB-IFEval) for evaluating implicit instruction-following in real-time spoken interaction. (DSB-IFEval) comprises 1,038 test cases spanning eight diverse assistant roles and evaluates five conditioning protocols for instruction-following: default behavior, explicit behavioral instructions, persona-implied behavior, combined persona--rule conditioning, and instruction conflict. We measure real-time floor management using a deterministic Instruction Adherence Score (IAS) and persona-consistent content using LLM-judged Persona Adherence Score (PAS). Across six real-time speech systems, we find architecture-dependent trade-offs. Full duplex models like F-Actor and PersonaPlex are more sensitive to whether conversational behavior is stated explicitly or must be inferred from a persona, with adherence dropping by 9.7% and 4.5%, respectively, under persona-only conditioning. In contrast, GPT-Realtime, MiniCPM-o, and Fun-Audio-Chat strongly adhere to persona-consistent content, but their floor behavior does not adapt across explicit and persona-only instructions and remains constrained on several proactive actions. We further find that even if systems reliably follow conflicting directives to their prescribed persona, they still struggle to override them under safety conflict. These results show that inferring the behavior implied by a role, executing it at the appropriate conversational moment, and resolving competing instructions remain distinct challenges for full-duplex voice agents.

</details>


### [115] [Feature Reconfiguration With Visual Prior for Medical Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03535)
*Yinan Liu,Jiankang Hong,Zhen Gao,Ye Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FreNet，一种基于视觉先验的特征重配置框架，在编码前进行像素级重配置（IPNN模块），在编码过程中进行特征级重配置（DFR模块），通过频率解耦和空间定位处理医学图像病变分割中的背景干扰和形态多样性问题，在9个医学图像分割基准上超越SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码器-解码器方法主要聚焦于增强特征提取或重新设计解码策略，但在编码阶段缺乏早期先验引导和特征重配置，难以有效应对病变分割中复杂背景干扰和多样病变形态两大挑战。

Method: FreNet包含两个核心模块：(1) Implicit Prior Neural Network (IPNN)，建模连续空间场并利用SAM的视觉先验在编码前对输入图像进行像素级重配置以抑制背景响应；(2) Dual-domain Feature Reconfiguration (DFR)模块，在编码过程中逐步重配置骨干网络特征，其中Frequency Decoupling Module (FDM)在频域解耦特征增强前后景判别性，Spatial Localization Module (SLM)在频域解耦后进行空间重定位以提升空间稳定性。

Result: 在涵盖三种成像模态的9个医学图像分割基准上，FreNet显著优于现有SOTA方法。在最具挑战性的ETIS数据集上，Dice系数较SOTA方法提升5.0%，较SAM提升7.2%。

Conclusion: 通过编码前的像素级视觉先验重配置和编码过程中的双域（频域+空间域）特征重配置策略，FreNet有效解决了医学病变分割中的背景干扰和形态多样性问题，验证了在编码阶段引入先验引导和特征重配置的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Lesion segmentation in medical images plays a critical role in clinical diagnosis and treatment planning. Despite significant advances, lesion segmentation remains challenging due to two major factors: (1) complex background interference; (2) diverse lesion morphology. Existing encoder-decoder based methods mainly focus on enhancing feature extraction or redesigning decoding strategies. However, they lack early prior guidance and feature reconfiguration during the encoding stage, limiting their effectiveness in handling these challenges. To address these limitations, we propose FreNet, a feature reconfiguration framework with visual priors, which performs pixel-level reconfiguration before encoding and feature-level reconfiguration during encoding for precise medical lesion segmentation. To suppress background responses, we propose an Implicit Prior Neural Network (IPNN), which models a continuous spatial field and leverages visual prior from SAM to reconfigure input image before encoding stage. To better handle diverse lesion morphology, we design a Dual-domain Feature Reconfiguration (DFR) module to progressively reconfigure backbone features during encoding stage. Within DFR, the Frequency Decoupling Module (FDM) decouples backbone features in frequency domain to enhance foreground-background discriminability, while the Spatial Localization Module (SLM) spatially relocates and improving spatial stability after frequency decoupling. Extensive experiments on 9 medical image segmentation benchmarks across three imaging modalities demonstrate that FreNet significantly outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods. On the challenging ETIS dataset, our method achieves Dice improvements of 5.0% over SOTA method and 7.2% over SAM.

</details>


### [116] [Do GUI Agents Know When Not to Act? Enabling Conflict-Aware Termination for Multimodal GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03438)
*Zhaoyuan Huang,Tianjie Ju,Pengzhou Cheng,Zheng Wu,Yansi Li,Chuanbiao Song,Jun Lan,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CONFLICTGUI基准测试，用于研究GUI智能体在遇到指令冲突（指令内部冲突与指令-GUI上下文冲突）时能否识别并终止执行。针对智能体存在的"执行偏见过度服从"问题，提出CONFLICTGUARD推理时框架，通过可行性验证协议与条件性动作调节机制，使智能体在冲突情境下转向终止行为，同时保持正常任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实用户可能因粗心发出不可执行的指令，但可靠的GUI智能体不仅要会执行，还要知道何时不应执行。现有智能体在可行任务上表现良好时，往往在冲突指令下仍盲目执行，存在严重的过度服从问题，缺乏冲突感知的终止能力。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：（1）CONFLICTGUI基准，覆盖指令内部冲突和指令-GUI上下文冲突两类场景；（2）CONFLICTGUARD推理时框架，包含可行性验证协议（引导智能体在执行前评估指令逻辑与GUI证据）和条件性动作调节机制（将过度服从的执行导向终止行为）。

Result: 在五个广泛使用的GUI智能体上进行的实验表明，CONFLICTGUARD显著提升了冲突任务的成功率，同时保持了正常的GUI任务性能不受影响。

Conclusion: 轻量级的推理时干预能够大幅提升GUI智能体识别不当执行场景并克制不必要操作的能力，证明了在不改变正常执行能力的前提下提高冲突感知终止能力是可行的。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents are increasingly used to execute natural-language instructions on user interfaces, yet real users may issue infeasible instructions due to benign mistakes. A reliable agent should not only know how to act, but also when not to act. In this work, we introduce CONFLICTGUI, a benchmark covering instruction-internal conflicts and instruction-GUI context conflicts to study conflict-aware termination. Our evaluation reveals severe execution-biased overcompliance: agents that perform well on feasible tasks often continue to execute blindly under conflicting instructions. To mitigate this behavior, we propose CONFLICTGUARD, an inference-time framework that aligns an agent's feasibility awareness with its action generation. CONFLICTGUARD contains two coupled components: a feasibility verification protocol that guides the agent to assess instruction logic and GUI-side evidence before acting, and a conditional action modulation mechanism that steers agents from over-compliant execution into termination-oriented behavior. Experiments across five widely-used agents demonstrate that CONFLICTGUARD improves average conflict task success rate significantly, while preserving normal GUI-task performance. These results validate that a lightweight inference-time intervention can substantially boost GUI Agent's competence to identify inappropriate execution scenarios and refrain from unnecessary actions.

</details>


### [117] [Beyond "Made with AI": Visualizing Provenance Density to Mitigate the Transparency Penalty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03460)
*Qing Zhang,Yifei Huang,Juyoung Lee,Thad Starner,Jun Rekimoto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"流畅性陷阱"问题——生成式AI让流畅文本变廉价后，用户无法再用语言流畅度判断真伪。为此提出"来源密度"（Provenance Density）证据可视化界面，展示文本中可验证主张的密度。用户研究（81人）显示该界面使真假辨别差距显著（+4.15分，d=1.82），而无法看到信号的对照组无任何辨别能力。技术审计（200样本）发现仅依靠检索密度不足，一致性否决（Consistency Veto）在动态查询中携带了大部分判别信号。结论是AI内容透明应从作者身份披露转向证据可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI使得用户不再能依赖文本流畅度作为真实性的代理指标，产生"流畅性陷阱"：用户既轻信流畅的幻觉内容，又会在内容被标记为AI生成后过度怀疑真实内容。现有"Made with AI"二元标签仅披露作者身份，却未展示支撑主张的证据，需要更有效的透明机制。

Method: 提出Provenance Density证据可视化界面，在文本中展示已验证主张的密度。开展两项研究：(1) 81人参与的用户研究，让参与者在理想化Provenance Density界面下与无信号条件下进行真假文本辨别；(2) 对200个样本的技术审计，检验检索密度与一致性否决（Consistency Veto）信号在动态查询场景中的判别能力。

Result: 用户研究显示理想化Provenance Density界面产生了较大的真伪辨别差距（+4.15点，Cohen's d=1.82），而无信号对照组无显著辨别能力。技术审计发现仅有检索密度不足以判别真伪，出乎意料的是，一致性否决在动态查询中承载了绝大部分判别信号。

Conclusion: 当AI生成内容与人类写作越来越难以区分时，有效的透明度机制应从作者身份披露（authorship disclosure）转向证据可视化（evidence visualization），帮助用户基于证据密度而非文本流畅度来判断内容可信度。

Abstract: As generative AI makes polished prose cheap to produce, users can no longer rely on fluency as a proxy for truth. We call this failure mode the Fluency Trap: users trust fluent hallucinations while also discounting accurate content once it is disclosed as AI-generated. Binary ``Made with AI'' labels respond with authorship disclosure, but they do not show what supports a claim. We propose Provenance Density, an evidence-visualization interface that shows the density of verified claims in a text. In a user study with 81 participants, an idealized Provenance Density interface produced a large discernment gap between truth and fabrication ($+4.15$ points, $d=1.82$), whereas participants given no signal showed no detectable discrimination. A technical audit with 200 samples shows that retrieval density alone is insufficient; unexpectedly, the Consistency Veto carries most of the discriminative signal on dynamic queries. As AI-generated content becomes indistinguishable from human writing, effective transparency must move from authorship disclosure toward evidence visualization.

</details>


### [118] [AutoGraphForge: Towards Automated Graph Theory Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03478)
*Ján Pastorek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为AutoGraphForge的自动化计算流水线，用于图论中的猜想生成-反驳-形式化-证明系统。该流水线以反例引导的方式迭代生成猜想，通过新颖性过滤器和约348,000个图的测试数据集筛选候选猜想，产生6,522个存活猜想（包括我们手工证明的关于消没数和边覆盖数的非平凡关系），随后将幸存猜想翻译成Lean 4语句并通过两个神经证明器进行内核验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图论中存在大量未证明的猜想，传统人工方式难以系统性地探索。开发者希望构建一个端到端的自动化系统，能够自主生成、筛选、反驳、形式化并证明图论猜想，从而加速数学发现和验证的闭环过程。

Method: 方法包括五个核心阶段：(1) 猜想生成：Graffiti3生成器基于小规模快照表提出猜想，仅由自身猜想的反例扩展；(2) 新颖性过滤：利用559条经典及民间的已知关系（在传递闭包和线性恒等式替换下封闭）通过线性规划判断候选猜想是否已被已知结果蕴含；(3) 数据集反例测试：针对约348,000个图的数据集（包括House of Graphs不变量导出、所有最多9个顶点的连通图穷举、若干极值族及随机模型）测试；(4) 主动搜索：用反例搜索算法进一步攻击；该循环运行多轮后在HPC集群上产生6,522个存活的猜想，其中包括人工证明的关于二部图和正则图中消没数与边覆盖数的非平凡关系；(5) 形式化证明阶段：将每个存活猜想确定性地翻译为Lean 4语句骨架，集成DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B（vLLM服务）和Lean特化的OProver-32B两个神经证明器，每个候选证明均通过内核对mathlib4及自定义不变量序言进行验证。

Result: 在HPC集群上运行多轮后，该流水线产生了6,522个经受住反例数据集、新颖性过滤器及所有主动搜索运行的猜想。其中包括关于二部图和正则图中消没数（annihilation number）与边覆盖数（edge-cover number）之间的非平凡关系，这些关系已由作者手工证明。形式化和证明阶段已端到端实现并通过初始健全性检查，完整流水线正在集群上运行。

Conclusion: AutoGraphForge展示了一个完整的自动化数学发现流水线的可行性：从反例引导的猜想生成，到大规模数据集过滤，再到Lean 4形式化验证结合神经证明器。该工作验证了将自动猜想、反驳、形式化和证明集成于统一计算框架的潜力，其中部分非平凡猜想已得到证明，系统正处于持续运行状态。

Abstract: We report on our ongoing project to develop a computational pipeline, AutoGraphForge, for an automated graph-theoretic conjecturing-refuting-formalizing-proving system. Conjecture generation is counterexample-guided and runs in rounds: a Graffiti3 generator proposes conjectures over a small, evolving snapshot table $T$ (initially a few hundred graphs with their computed invariants) that grows only by counterexamples to its own conjectures. A novelty filter of $559$ classical and folklore relations, closed under transitive composition and linear identity substitution, decides via a linear program whether a candidate is already implied by known results. Surviving candidates are tested against a dataset of about $348,000$ graphs, unioning the complete House of Graphs invariant export, the exhaustive census of all connected graphs on at most nine vertices, several extremal families (strongly regular, minimal Ramsey, Cayley, cages, barbells, lollipops, spiders), and random models. Counterexample-search algorithms then attack the remainder. Run for several rounds on an HPC cluster, the loop yields $6,522$ conjectures that survived the refutation dataset, the novelty filter and every active-search run -- among them nontrivial relations between the annihilation number and the edge-cover number for bipartite and regular graphs, which we prove by hand. A subsequent formalization and proving stage deterministically translates each surviving conjecture into a Lean 4 statement skeleton; every candidate proof is kernel-verified against a pinned mathlib4 and our custom invariant preamble. This stage integrates two neural provers -- DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B (served with vLLM) and the Lean-specialised OProver-32B -- behind the independent kernel check. It is implemented end-to-end and passes initial sanity checks, with the full pipeline currently running on the cluster.

</details>


### [119] [Making Every Tool Call Count: Necessary Tool-Evidence Path Rewards for Agentic Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03493)
*Xingming Long,Yu Liu,Zhiwei Yang,Hanqi Feng,Shaojie Zhang,Barnabas Poczos,Chao Jiang,Zhenbo Luo,Lei Jiang,Pei Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NTEP（Necessary Tool-Evidence Path）注解方案及对应的NTEP-R奖励机制，用于监督智能体视觉语言模型（VLM）的工具调用过程，确保每次工具调用都严格推进推理链路。通过在8B参数模型上实现NTEP-8B，在七个图像接地基准上显著提升了面向搜索的准确率和工具使用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体VLM训练主要基于最终答案正确性评估工具使用，缺乏对证据获取和使用过程的细粒度监督，导致两个关键缺陷：(i)模型频繁发出冗余或偏离目标的工具调用，无法收集必要证据；(ii)即使调用了合适工具，模型也常常无法从观察结果中提取所需信息。

Method: (1)提出NTEP注解方案，为每个查询明确指定必要的外部证据及对应的工具调用序列；(2)提出NTEP-R（NTEP Reward）监督机制，奖励模型在调用前意图与必要证据获取目标对齐、以及在调用后从观察中总结的信息与必要证据对齐；(3)引入非重复目标正则化器，惩罚重复访问已满足的NTEP目标所产生的冗余调用。

Result: 在七个图像接地基准上的广泛评估表明，8B参数的NTEP-8B在统一三工具框架中显著提升了搜索导向的准确率和工具使用效率。

Conclusion: 结果表明细粒度的工具-证据路径监督对于训练稳健的智能体VLM具有关键价值，弥补了以往仅依赖最终答案正确性进行训练监督的不足。

Abstract: Modern vision-language models (VLMs) can directly answer many image-grounded questions, yet they often struggle with complex queries requiring fine-grained visual details or external knowledge. To acquire this missing evidence, agentic VLMs invoke tools such as image cropping, image search, and text search. However, existing training paradigms primarily evaluate tool-use based on final answer correctness, leaving evidence acquisition and utilization insufficiently supervised. This leads to two critical shortcomings: (i) models frequently issue redundant or off-target tool calls that fail to gather necessary evidence, and (ii) even when appropriate tools are called, models often fail to extract the necessary information from the resulting observations. To address these limitations, we introduce the NTEP (Necessary Tool-Evidence Path), a novel annotation scheme that explicitly specifies the essential external evidence and corresponding tool calls for each query. Building upon this, we propose NTEP-R (NTEP Reward), a supervision mechanism ensuring that each tool invocation strictly advances the reasoning process toward the final solution. Specifically, our approach rewards the agent for aligning its pre-call intent with a necessary evidence-seeking goal, and for ensuring the information summarized from the post-call observation aligns with the necessary evidence. Furthermore, we introduce a non-repeated-goal regularizer to penalize redundant calls that revisit satisfied NTEP goals. Extensive evaluations on seven image-grounded benchmarks demonstrate that our 8B-parameter instantiation, NTEP-8B, significantly improves both search-oriented accuracy and tool-use efficiency within a unified three-tool framework. These results highlight the critical value of fine-grained tool-evidence path supervision for training robust agentic VLMs.

</details>


### [120] [GrowPage: On-Demand KV Budgeting for Efficient LLM Reasoning Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03494)
*Qiankun Ma,Yanjiang Zhou,Zinan Xiong,Haofei Wang,Zhen Song,Yang Xiang,Ziyao Zhang,Hairong Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GrowPage是一个按需KV缓存预算框架，将KV容量视为运行时资源，通过双时间尺度查询摘要估计注意力需求的变化，在容量边界时动态决定压缩现有KV状态或获取额外物理页，从而突破传统固定的每请求预算限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长输出推理使得KV缓存成为LLM服务的关键内存瓶颈。现有KV压缩方法依赖预定义的每请求预算，仅调整保留哪些KV状态，总容量在整个解码过程中固定不变。然而推理工作负载存在巨大的需求变化：不同请求需要不同的KV容量，且单个请求的注意力需求在生成过程中会动态演化。

Method: GrowPage维护轻量级的双时间尺度查询摘要，分别捕获近期和长期的注意力行为，利用它们之间的注意力工作集来估计需求演化。在每个容量边界处，GrowPage要么在当前分配内压缩KV状态，要么在出现更广泛的注意力需求时获取额外的物理页。该方法与PagedAttention的页级内存抽象集成，保持连续批处理和前缀缓存。
